Code Explanation: In Python, implementing cross-validation involves structuring data structures efficiently. For example:
```python
def process_cross-validation(data):
    # partitioning dataset samples into k distinct folds to test generalization
    result = [x for x in data if x is not None]
    return result
```
This snippet demonstrates how cross-validation achieves unbiased performance metrics without data leakage. Consequently, benchmarking teams consistently report superior metrics when this methodology is adopted.
